## Ash ledger (Handfuls; a clean Flask = 8)

**At the gate, D+41:**
- Road: 1 Flask (8), on the cart.
- Inside: 8 Flasks (64), in Jab's frame.
- Home: 3 Flasks (24), on the cart.
- Settled: Callie 1, Toren 1, Dee 1. (Dee's Handful is first stated in Callie's D+41 count.)
- Jab carries no settled ash (his own reserve is "a hand and a bit", floor a finger). Rook works off his own, at half-turns.

**Road jar:**

| When | Spent on | Road jar after |
|---|---|---:|
| D+41 | Callie re-settles ¼ spent on Ward-on-self practice | 7¾ |
| D+41 night | Toren's dark-lane heel light, off his **own** reserve | 7¾ |
| D+42 | Toren re-settles ¼ spent on the washout sets | 7½ |
| D+43 | The copper man (Jab: 2, plus vent loss and Dee's draw of his leftovers) | 5¼ |
| D+44 | Callie re-settles ¾ spent on lee practice (⅛ plant, ¹⁄₁₀ first roll, 5 × ¹⁄₂₀, ¹⁄₁₀ for the fifth-halt three, 3 final tries about ¹⁄₂₀ each) | 4½ |
| D+45 | Toren re-settles ½ (gap sets) → 4. Drift drill: Jab on Rook ½, Callie re-settles ¼, Dee re-settles ¼. Dee: "a Handful off the road because you went to look. Toren's half is the gaps." | 3 |
| D+46 | All of it on Pell in the drift, vented into the drift | **0** |

Callie's summary at the wash: 5 spent in five days — 2¼ on the ditch, 1 on the drift, the rest (1¾) on learning; "not a grain on a hound". Dee: "we got to the wash with three in the road jar".

**Where the road jar rides:** in the cart's rack **behind the driver's bench**, with the Flasks (ch 27, 29). Jab's frame rack rests on the tailboard; the road jar never does.

**Inside jars on D+46:**
- **Jar A**, opened by Dee from the lip on Jab's call ("Open it. It's mine to say"). About 3 went on Pell in the drift, including vent loss ("Five. Near enough. Three went on him in the drift"). The rest was spent in the cut: Dee on Pell (to 14, about 1½) and on Toren (from 60, about 1½), Jab on Callie (to 12, about ½), and **the first of Rook** (about 1½; "off what was left of the first jar… and ran low. He said *jar* before it was dry"). Empty. Per-patient splits are estimates; the page states only the drift's 3 and the order.
- **Jar B**, the end jar of the top row nearest the rail's end, broke against Tonk's rail when a husk knocked Jab down. All 8 was lost, clean, down his back into the drift. The rail stopped the rest of the top row going over his head. On the afternoon of D+46 this pale streak is seen moving uphill with everything else.
- **Jar C**, opened in the cut:

| Spent on | Handfuls |
|---|---:|
| Rook, the rest (the drift grey in the reopened wound, drawn by Jab) | 3 |
| Dee (by Jab; she lay 15 min on the lip in the wind) | 1 |
| Jab's leftovers (by Dee) | ½ |
| Duchess | ½ |
| The night's settling: Callie, Toren and Dee, 1 each | 3 |

Jar C is empty after the settling.
- **Remaining:** 5 full Flasks = **40** (stated on the page: "Five left. Whole. Forty").
- The abort line stays at **16**, leaving **24 to work with against the 48 priced**. The deep rooms alone were priced at 30 (two hours, five people).

**Home:** 3 Flasks (24), untouched, on the cart. They were priced for a six-day road that is now longer (see clocks).

**Floor checks in the cut (ch 32, before rotation), abort rule answered:**
- **Jab:** "A hand. Floor's a finger. You never went under… We don't turn round on you." The jar was always there before his own reserve was.
- **Toren:** stone gone; own "under half". His account: the lean from the crown to the post, "Twenty turns, near enough. The stone for most of it. My own for the drop and the two struts, and the steps, and the rail." Arm "Jarred. At the drop. Same place. It stayed in." Dee: "It stays in till Sowerby."
- **Callie:** stone gone; "Your own's on the floor. Not under it." "I stopped."
- **Dee:** her settled Handful untouched at the check; "It'll go on the watch today" (the watch draws leftovers, as in the pen). "Nobody's under. Nobody turns us round on a floor tonight."
- **Rook:** off (separately: 12 glass turns lit, two over his half working turn).

**Settled at exit:** Callie's settling is shown. Toren's and Dee's Handfuls are announced for the night ("Tonight we settle… a Handful each, out of the open jar") and paid from jar C in Callie's ledger, but the page does not show them finishing. Toren's own reserve (under half, less one afternoon set) and Callie's own (at her floor) come back with sleep, not ash. Movement Five should open with Toren full, or say otherwise on purpose; he is the slowest settler, at most of an hour a Handful. Toren spent one more set of his own in the afternoon bringing the cart off the crown ("three breaths of a wheel").

**Vent jars:** six at the gate.
- #1 was sealed and left in the siding ditch on D+43.
- On the D+45 drill, in the wash and in the cut, healers vented into already-dirty ground or stone, not into jars.
- **Five** remain on the cart.

**Where the emptied jars are:** not stated. The emptied road jar was under Dee's arm at the cut. Jars A and C were emptied in the cut. Jar B's socket has broken glass in it.

## The wash (D+46), as staged

**Terrain:**
- The last slope of the old line drops east to west onto a 250-pace embankment across the wash's bottom. The embankment is a man's height and a half high, with a bed four paces wide of packed stone. Iron posts stand along its north edge every 20 paces.
- North side: the wash's bed, full of grey ash drifts (off the hill, contaminated). They are deepest against the embankment's north foot, half as high as the bank, around the culvert.
- South side: a dead reed marsh standing in black water, running to the river. It is "the dark side".
- The culvert is a stone arch 180 paces from the east end and 70 from the west. The south face fell out long ago and the arch settled onto the fall, so that half is "sitting on stone". The north side has been undercut by the wash, so the crown hangs on that side and is cracked across nearly to the south edge. An iron post stands in a lead collar in the north parapet's ring stone and leans out over the wash.
- The west end runs into a cut through a low ridge. The counter reads under the red at the cut's mouth and about 30 twenty paces in.
- The pump house is 1 mile beyond.

**Counter readings:**
- D+45 dusk scouting: 14 at the east end; 40 in gusts; a guessed "a hundred, maybe" at the drift foot by the arch.
- D+46 at dawn with no wind: 12 at the start, then 20, 26 and 30.
- In the gust: 60 inside the lee and 90 outside it. After Rook's lip kills, 90 inside and 120 outside.
- At the lip: 110, 120, then 140 after Jab's kill. Buzz, red, after the fourth husk.

**Wind:** west-north-west off the hill, blowing the drifts across the bed. It was down at dawn, returned at about 100 paces, and dropped again at midday.

**Positions at the start (and what was carried):**
- Callie on the north (windward) edge with the hook and the second counter, carrying a settled Handful.
- Pell at Duchess's head on the south side, knife on his belt.
- The cart in the middle of the bed, carrying 3 home jars, the empty lead carrier and 5 vent jars.
- Jab at the tailboard on the north side, with 8 fence jars in the frame.
- Dee beside Jab with the glass and the road jar (3) in her arm, and a settled Handful.
- Rook at the tail on the south side, to fence the marsh; half a turn.
- Toren on the south side by the north axle, carrying a full settled Handful.

**Exits:** back east (250 paces, reading 14) until the arch; forward west (70 paces to the cut) after it. Never the reeds (chest-deep) and never the drifts.

**Sequence (glass turns are minutes; Rook's lit time is the clock before the red, Dee's glass after it):**
1. At about 100 paces the wind returns. "Lane"; the lee runs in the gusts.
2. Three husks rise out of the drifts and come at the lane. Rook crosses to the north edge with his blade lit and kills two on the lip; the wind throws the grey over everyone. Dee: "Not in the wind."
3. About two glass turns after Rook lights, the north wheel reaches the crack. Toren sets from a bed slab on the south half ("Set"). The north half of the crown drops and the cart hangs on his strut ("Holding"). The wheel in the hole takes some of it; the strut carries **the lean**, "less than a wheel on the gaps, not much less", and it does not roll past. He counts breaths under it. Duchess screams in the shafts.
4. Two hounds come out of the reeds at the dark under the cart. Rook recrosses and fences one; the other goes round.
5. Pell, dragged onto the north half with Duchess, cuts both traces at the right place. Duchess bolts east. The rotten north edge tips Pell off the bank into the drift, breaking his right ankle on the arch's wing wall. His knife goes into the wash.
6. Jab goes down the bank into the drift to Pell; Dee orders "Nobody else comes down". Callie twice fails to roll the lee down the loose bank ("There's no still").
7. Dee lies on her belly on the lip and reaches the road jar down to Jab's left hand, his back to the bank. He runs draw ten, vent three into the drift downwind.
8. The third husk comes along the bank foot at Jab's hands. He goes to let the knife come, finds his left hand on the jar, takes it off, and only then lets the knife come on his left fist. He kills the husk with the lit hook, and the grey sifts over Pell ("Every one you kill down there goes on him").
9. The second hound goes under the cart and hits Rook high in the back, reopening the old wound. Rook kills it. He has been lit 12 glass turns, two over his half working turn. Dee: "You're off… That's the Director's word, and it's mine." He sits. Dee: "Toren. Say it." Toren: "Holding. Half the stone gone, and a bit. It's the lean, not the wheel… The slab keeps moving. I keep catching it." (About ten glass turns under the lean.)
10. Jab calls *jar* before the road jar (3) is dry. "Then the frame." Dee: "That's the fence's… it comes off the forty-eight." Jab: "Open it… It's mine to say." Dee lifts jar A out of his rack from the lip.
11. At the fourth draw Jab's palm goes warm (lodging). He calls *vent* himself and vents 8, then 10 breaths. Dee cannot rotate him: Rook is off, and Dee must stay clean as watch. Jab: "You come down, there's nobody clean."
12. The fourth husk hits Jab at the hip. He falls forward over Pell like a sack, hands kept where they were. Jar B breaks on the rail and its clean ash runs down his back into the drift. Callie hands the Ward over to Edge on a count of two and cuts the husk from the lip; its grey falls on them. Buzz.
13. Callie taps the stuck needle once: true red, a body trapped. Dee names **Jab** (Pell), **Toren** (the cart) and **Callie** (the lane, "Not down the bank… along the top… lean it"). She takes Rook out and they go west to the cut. The glass runs from the red; Dee calls **every** turn from the cut with the three names, and Callie hears only some ("The wind would have perhaps one in two").
14. Callie, on the lip a pace east of the broken crown (her side catching as she leans), holds a *still* lean-to lee off the curve on the lip's stone, over Jab and Pell and not over herself. Standing still costs less than walking but more than a door ("thinner and longer, over two bodies… harder than the hook ever had"). She has about half her settled Handful ("not much more than three turns of the glass" at that price), so she runs the plan from the fire: **lane** when she sees a gust lift the grey off the drifts upwind, **down** in the lulls (5–20 breaths). Jab draws the same in lulls; the lee does nothing about the ground.
15. Toren: "The stone's under a quarter… Every time, I catch it, and catching's dear." The slab shifts. Callie: "Ground… the ring stone… cut… on the springing on the good side." He lets go for a hand's drop (left arm jarred, "Same place… It's in") and re-sets on the ring stone: "It's not moving." The catch empties his stone.
16. Callie hears the call for turn **2**. Toren: "The stone's gone… I'm on my own now. Two turns of the glass of that, and a bit, and then I'm at my floor." The post stands on the north parapet **two hands' widths west of the cart's front corner**. Toren sets a second strut on the ring stone ("twice the price") and walks the front corner two hand-widths west onto the post ("Post's got it"). He looks down: "Stone's gone. Half my own. A turn of the glass left in it, and a bit." Callie hears turn **3**. Total under the lean, crown to post: about twenty glass turns.
17. Toren sets strut-steps down the bank on buried stones (his own weight, a breath each, paid from his own). Three inside; in a lull Callie reads "A quarter of the stone. Less." Toren and Jab lift Pell; Callie rolls the lee west along the lip, still lane/down to the gusts.
18. At 30 paces two more husks rise. Callie takes Toren out of the lee so it holds on two, and keeps it up continuously till the husks are done. Toren kills both against the lee's outside; the grey slides down the far side and over him.
19. Toren back under. At about 50 paces the stone is a scrap; in the next gust it goes and Callie keeps the lee up **off her own water**, which costs her cold hands and feet and a thin high sound in her ears. At the cut mouth the bank is chest-high: a strut-step and rail up (Toren's own); Jab and Dee pull Pell up.
20. With everyone out, Callie is at her floor, not under it: "I'm at my floor. I'm stopping." She walks the last ten paces with the hook as a stick: 90 at the cut mouth, under the red. Dee: "Fifteen turns… in the red." Callie's legs sit her down; nosebleed.

**Kills:** six husks (Rook 2 on the lip, Jab 1 and Callie 1 at the foot under the arch, Toren 2 further west against the lee) and two hounds (Rook). No spawn reconstitutes on the page.

**After the fight:**
- In the cut: floor checks first (above). Dee belts Pell's broken ankle to his good one before drawing him. Dee works first, Jab watches and Callie is "a reserve with nothing". The order of work: Pell, Toren, Callie, Rook (jar A, then jar C), Dee, then Jab's leftovers. Rook is bound with **the last of the clean cloth**.
- Midday: the wind drops. Callie and Toren are named to go back. The ash rhythm is observed.
- Afternoon: rope traces (Pell directing, ankle still belted). Toren sets under the north wheel on the ring stone ("Set" / "Rolling" from Callie), one set off his own, and the cart comes out west.
- After the collapse and Duchess's draw, Dee splints Pell at the cut mouth with two pieces of the tailboard rail Toren pried off, **bound with strips of the cart's canvas** (the clean cloth was all used on Rook).
- Three paces after Toren steps off, the north half and the arch collapse into the wash with the post. There is now a 4-pace gap in the embankment; "That was the road."

## The turn — what the party can now act on

- **Observed by all, with no wind:** on the afternoon of D+46 loose ash moves west, up the wash's grade, toward the hill, in fine threads. This includes the pale streak of jar B's spilled clean ash, all six husk smears and the two hound smears on the bed. It moves for about ten breaths and stops for about three, "a hand's width" each pulse.
- **Callie's counter over the drift** pulses on the same beat, between 80 and 90: "up for about ten… down for three."
- **The edge of the sky patch** over the hill's flat top thickens and thins on the same beat, and is seen again that night.
- **Jab's inference, stated as inference:**
  - With his palm on clean stone he feels a pull that is "not a lean", with "nobody in it". It comes from the west and up. "It did not haul."
  - "Something's lifting it. Up there. Under that. At the middle of it… It's lifting like we lift. Ten breaths, and it stops for three, like you taught me to vent… It's doing what I do." Dee adds: "To ash." Jab: "Only ash," which he cannot know.
  - "I don't know what it is… I can't feel it."
- **Rook:** "I've never seen ash go uphill." **Dee:** "Nobody touch it. Nobody go after it."
- **The trap, stated by Callie and Toren:** there is no loose ash left at the edge to lift, because "It's all going in". The ash they would need past the first room is under the sky patch, and so are the boxes. "It was always the same place."

## Bodies at exit (D+46 night)

- **Pell:**
  - Right ankle broken on the wing wall. Belted to the good leg in the cut in the morning; splinted in the afternoon with two pieces of the cart's tailboard rail bound with strips of the cart's canvas. He cannot walk and lies on the cart.
  - Swallowed and breathed drift. Drawn from the road jar (3) plus about 4½ Handfuls of jar A to 14 at the chest.
  - Lost his knife in the wash.
  - He was always to stay outside the fence, and still does.
- **Rook:**
  - The old back wound was reopened high between the shoulders by the second hound, with drift grey driven in. Dee washed it with her whole water skin, Jab drew the grey (the end of jar A, about 1½, then jar C, about 3), Dee stitched it with a curved needle and bound it with the last clean cloth.
  - "It'll be hot tonight. Tomorrow we'll know."
  - Two turns over his half-turn. **Off "till I say"; two healers from now on.**
  - Could not rise in one on D+42 (three movements); rose in two at the cut.
- **Toren:**
  - Left forearm jarred on D+45 ("Same place. Not as bad") and again at the ring-stone drop on D+46 ("Same place… It stayed in"). Still in its cuff, never reset and never used to lever or catch; Dee: "It stays in till Sowerby."
  - The wash cost: settled stone emptied under the lean (about twenty glass turns from the crown to the post); own reserve "under half" after the drop, two struts, post, three strut-steps and the rail, plus one afternoon set. Named: Guard (Rook, ch 32).
  - Fell like a sack in the washout stream on D+42.
  - Dusted outside the lee while killing two husks (60 at the chest), then drawn by Dee.
  - Own reserve was down about a turn after the D+41 night and back by noon on D+42.
  - A full settling of stone is planned for the night; his own comes back with sleep.
- **Callie:**
  - Left ribs bruised again on D+44 (her own fixed hook): "not cracked again… a week". A twinge when she leans the hook onto the lip on D+46.
  - Nosebleeds on D+44 (the whole-cart lee) and D+46 (after the wash lee).
  - Exposed on the lip outside her own lee, then drawn to 12.
  - Settled stone emptied; her own water spent to her floor, not under it, in the last stretch to the cut (cold hands and feet, ringing ears, legs give at the cut). "I'm at my floor. I'm stopping." Comes back with sleep; she settles a Handful that night.
- **Jab:**
  - Palm lodging from the fourth draw in the drift; he vented long. Dee took the leftovers in the cut ("palms went cool"), but they are "still a little warm" at the fire.
  - Knocked down at the hip by a husk; "not hurt", as far as he knew.
  - Floor measured in the cut: "A hand. Floor's a finger. You never went under." No turn-round trigger.
- **Dee:** lay 15 min on the lip in the wind and was drawn by Jab from jar C. No injury. Settled Handful intact at the floor check, spent on the watch in the cut, re-settled that night. Water skin empty; last clean cloth used on Rook.
- **Duchess:** sound. Bolted east at a gallop, stood waiting at the foot of the slope, was led back by Callie, and was drawn by Jab (½). Traces cut, now rope ("the knots were bad and Pell said they would do").
- **The copper man (one-off, unnamed):** hands "done"; chest 60→16; sick again expected. Gone east with the girl and the copper, minus three coils.

## Object state at exit

- **Cart:**
  - West of the collapsed crown, at the pump house.
  - Carries the 3 home Flasks, the empty lead carrier on straw, 5 vent jars, canvas and gear.
  - The right hub was greased on D+44. Traces are rope. Part of the tailboard rail is gone (the splint); some of the canvas is cut into splint strips.
  - The road jar (empty) and the Flasks ride in the rack behind the driver's bench.
  - Water skins aboard (not counted on the page).
- **Jab's frame:**
  - 5 full fence Flasks. Jar B's socket (the top row, rail end) has broken glass in it.
  - Tonk's curved ash rail held; it took the fall.
  - Emptied jars A and C are not placed on the page.
- **Jab's coat:** a pale stain down the back from jar B, shaken out downwind at the river.
- **Little:**
  - Checked at the river: "Seven. The room… It's clean."
  - In Jab's coat, left side, buttoned. He holds it that night while counting the sky.
- **Callie's hook:** dusted at the wash. Its washing is not shown (the grey coats' tag *pen, 35th* is still on it).
- **The second counter:** with Callie. The needle stuck at the top of the red once and was tapped once.
- **Dee:** brass glass, book (the D+43 and D+44 entries, the drill words, the wash times "fifteen turns"), her small bag with needle and thread. Water skin empty.
- **Pell:** knife lost in the wash. The atlas (oilcloth) and route sheet are not located on the page after the wash.
- **Toren's signed paper, back side:**
  - Watches and a nightly count from D+41 night to D+46 ("Out: 6. In: 6."), plus "Tomorrow's Gran's" (D+42) and "Gran's was today" (D+43).
  - The copper-man entry.
  - The wash plan and exits.
  - Callie's account of who was inside the lee.
  - The D+46 record: "*The road east is cut.*" and "*The ash goes uphill. Ten and three… The same count as the sky.*"
  - The copied plate and the stone are in his coat.
- **Left on the road:** vent jar #1 sealed in the siding ditch with three hot coils and a chalk-crossed stone.
- **The wash culvert:** gone. North half, arch and iron post fell into the wash; a 4-pace gap in the embankment.
- **The pump house:** roofless. Pipes run into the river. It stands on gravel on the east bank.
- **The east gate:** a three-plank footbridge with a rail on one side, standing. At the far end a chained iron-framed wire gate. The fence runs north along the far bank.

## Knowledge boundaries at exit

- **Known:** the ten-and-three recall of *loose* ash toward something at or under the rip's centre; the shared counter and sky evidence; that it is not a person Jab can "feel".
- **Not known and not shown:**
  - what the thing is, or whether it is one creature;
  - whether it repairs or reconstitutes spawn (the packet's M5 mechanic is not dramatised: every kill stayed down);
  - whether it can draw ash that is settled or jarred. Jab's "only ash… the loose ash" is his guess. Jar B's ash moved because it was spilled and loose.
- **Jab's reach:** he felt "somebody bad" at about a mile on D+43. Dee remarks on it and nobody explains it. **Owner ruling:** one unexplained datum, not a general rule.
- **Withheld:**
  - Dee's door count and history beyond the canon of the wire and the two months: kept to "Then for a long time I didn't see him."
  - Rook's third door.
  - Katori's statement.
  - Disks, Cinder, the Makers.
  - The inside of the fence, the stair and the boxes: not entered.
- **The Director's narrow permission is quoted twice (ch 32)** and has not been broken. The party has not entered the rip or gone looking for what is under it. It "is not coming to us" (Rook); "It's taking the ash to it."

## Open clocks and exact exit pressure for Movement Five

- **Where and when:** the pump house on the east bank, outside the fence, the night of D+46. Six days out, on the original schedule. The east gate is across the footbridge.
- **Ash:**
  - 40 inside after the night's settling; line 16; 24 to work with (priced 48; the rooms alone 30). Two healers, since Rook is off.
  - Callie: 24 "doesn't get five people down the stair and into one room and back up it. Not with two healers. Not deep."
  - Any more ash must come from where the loose ash is going, under the patch, with the boxes.
- **Home:** 24, untouched. The road east is cut at the wash.
  - Options (Pell):
    - on foot across the wash bottom, quick and washing after, which the cart cannot do;
    - north round the drifts' head toward the hill ("where it's worse");
    - south through the marsh to the river and down this bank.
  - "Two days more, maybe, whichever way. With me on the cart and not walking."
- **Machine:**
  - Senna's D+43 cycle happened off-page; it was marked, not witnessed.
  - The next cycle is about D+54, with the tech "guessing". The earliest return is now **D+55** (Toren). "Guessing isn't stopping"; Sowerby can dose off her blood "for a few cycles. Badly."
- **Bodies:**
  - Rook's back infection risk, known on D+47.
  - Pell cannot walk; he stays at the pump house and cart, as planned.
  - Jab's floor measured in the cut (a hand, floor a finger); his palms are still a little warm.
  - Callie's ribs; her own reserve at the floor overnight.
  - Toren's forearm (jarred twice; for Sowerby).
- **Meridian (unchanged, not visited):**
  - Reviews held over, clocks stopped at the gate.
  - Wyck cleared D+56.
  - The tech's hand known at about D+57.
  - Tonk and Tilda as at D+41.
  - Dessa's six blank lines for *back*.
- **Decision deferred to the morning of D+47:** "Tomorrow we decide. With the light, and the counter, and Pell, and the sky where we can see it." The choice to cross the footbridge, and how, belongs to Movement Five.

## Deliberately absent or withheld

- **No new continuing named character.** The copper man and girl are unnamed one-offs and refuse to give names ("Don't"). Duchess and Pell are existing.
- **No Sustain-echo creature on the page, no reconstitution, no rip entry, no fence entry.**
- **No group Ward, no unlimited Guard, no ash creation, no miraculous healing.**
  - Sustain draws only active burden.
  - Fixed damage is left: the copper man's hands, Pell's ankle, Rook's tear.
  - Rook's own request "Draw my back. The pain" is refused: "That's torn… I can't draw a tear."
- **No romance.**
- **Owner-level conflicts untouched:** Wyck's side and finger count (Wyck appears only in memory: "Not that hand"), Toren's original-break wording, Tonk's week count, and Norris (not mentioned).

## Length and viewpoint (facts for review, not scores)

- **Length:** 54,991 words (`wc -w`) after repair (53,750 in the frozen first draft). **Owner ruling:** the map's 48–55k allowance governs this movement, not the packet's 42–50k. No developed action was cut.
  - The lowest-cost cuts, if wanted:
    - some of the camp-design argument in ch 26;
    - the middle of the gap practice in ch 29;
    - the opening rotation in ch 32, which the wash section's own staging partly repeats.
- **Viewpoint share, Movement Four (after repair):** Jab 36.8% (3 chapters), Callie 34.3% (3), Toren 28.9% (2).
  - **Owner ruling:** the allocation is accepted. No scenes were added or moved. Movement Five returns to an even three-way rotation.
- **Cumulative through Movement Four** (222,801 words of manuscript by `wc`, after repair): Callie 34.0%, Jab 33.0%, Toren 33.0%.
- **Scene breaks:** 107 `---` markers across 8 chapters, about 467 words per section. This is below the formula's ~950-word pause proxy, the same direction flagged in Movements Two and Three.
- **Formula comparison** (sentence distribution, Flesch, development-beat cadence, progression-vocabulary third placement) was **not run** in this session; it belongs to the editorial pass.
- **Tics watched:** none of "the woman with the good boots", "face did nothing at all, which was how", "tipped plate", "about four seconds". "Grey-haired man" is not used; the technician appears as "the man with the white hand" and "the technician".

# Movement Five — continuity checkpoint

Author run: Claude Opus 5.5 (`claude-opus-5-5`), Monroe Jackson 1.3.0 / O'Connor 1.3.0.
This is a working note for movement review and Movement Six planning. It is not
manuscript text and it is not an editorial score. It records the state the first-draft
page leaves behind, including what the page does *not* settle. Every number below was
checked against `manuscript/chapter-33.md` … `chapter-40.md` as drafted
(frozen copy: `editions/movement-005-first-draft/`).

## Conflicts resolved before drafting

| Question | Resolution on the page | Authority |
|---|---|---|
| Where the boxes are | Ch 18 puts the wheel boxes "in the racks on the left walls"; ch 14 says the crew "threw [them] on the floor… hundreds… where we dropped them". **Both kept:** the left-wall racks stand with their backs torn off, and the boxes lie heaped on the floor below them (ch 34) | Senna, chs 14 and 18 |
| Which "long corridor" | Senna uses the phrase for both levels. **Resolved:** a ground-floor corridor runs south along the long building; the yellow stair goes down off it on the left; at the bottom, the steel door; beyond it "the long corridor" runs **west** under the building and on under the hill's top toward the drum. Rooms are on its left (south) side (ch 34) | Chs 18, 21, 24 |
| What "into it" means for the Director's narrow permission | **The rip is the drum** — the round, broken-topped wall behind the long building, under the sky patch. The long building, the stair and the first rooms are the approved salvage route. Dee's written boundary: **nobody past the third door** (ch 33). The party never passed the first door. On D+47 the creature came east along its rope to them; on D+48 it was lying in room 1 on the carrier. The Director's third clause ("If it comes to you…") is quoted by Rook and applied (ch 36) | Ch 21 permission; packet |
| How a box reaches the cart without being carried | Pell's **tailboard sledge** with front and back ropes (ch 33); the shed's **long tongs**, found inside the empty carrier with a label *For the boxes. Not your hands.* (ch 33); a Guard strut as a **ramp** from the floor to the carrier's lip (chs 34, 39); a **barrow ramp** beside the yellow stair with iron rope rings at the top and the landing (ch 34); Pell's **tip-the-cart** loading (ch 39). Nobody lifted a box in their arms | Senna's rule; packet |
| Water | Dee's skin empty. Cart skins counted: **one full, one a third** (ch 33). River read above the pump house: **7, the room**; below it: 9. Drinking water boiled over flood-wrack gathered along the east bank — **enough for two pots a day** (ch 33). Nothing drunk inside the fence | Packet ("never a convenient unlimited supply") |
| Toren's settled state at entry | He finished settling jar C's Handful after Jab lay down on D+46 (most of an hour). Stone full on D+47 (ch 33) | M4 checkpoint note |
| Where emptied jars A and C are | In Jab's frame, stoppered, in their sockets (stated in Callie's D+47 count, ch 33). The road jar is empty in the cart rack | M4 left unplaced |

Callie at the top of the road: D+47 in, 1 in 12 counts; out, 2 in 12. Pell from the cart by heartbeat (60 to a glass turn): **ten and three all day; 7 falls in about four hours, more in the afternoon ("not sure")**. D+48 in: 1 in 12. D+48 out, after the kill: **no count** — eight and five, twelve and two, fourteen and nothing — and one fall at no three. Pell overnight: still no count | 34, 36, 37, 39, 40 |

| A frame on a cabinet | Inside room 1 Jab backs the frame onto a cabinet top: "a shelf, not a floor" | 38 |

## The lifter (the Sustain-echo Riftspawn)

Named by Jab's description and Rook's word ("It's got my job"), written by Toren as
**the lifter** (ch 36). A creature term, not a continuing named character.

- **Body:** a low grey heap, at first waist-high and two of Callie long, with a hump
  along its back; no face. At every three a line of light the colour of the pond-light
  opens along the top of the hump — the vent. It moves only by drawing itself along its
  own rope ("the way a man pulls himself along a rope hand over hand, only there were no
  hands"). It lays new rope behind it as it goes (the floor rope, ch 37).
- **Ropes:** grey ash in the cable trays and along floors, from finger-thick strands to
  thigh-thick; in room 1 the strands lie among the boxes where the copper was stripped.
  Three arm-ropes near the body lift and swing like wet rope at worked things within a
  few paces.
- **Mechanic observed on the page:** it **draws loose ash for ten breaths** (threads
  off floors and up walls into the ropes, the whole rope creeping west) and at the
  **three pushes** into any "hole": a cut rope, a heap (which stands up as a husk), a
  wound in itself, or **a hand that is drawing on it**. Killed husks lie for ten
  breaths, are drawn in, and stand up again at the three. Cut strands grow back
  together at the three. It never reached into a stoppered jar or the frame's jars;
  ash lifted and stoppered in a vent jar did not come back (the next husk stood up "a
  hand short").
- **Husks were its hands:** when it died, every husk it had made came apart on its own,
  in the room and in the corridor.
- **Yield (grading, ch 40):** Rook — *its own: a Flask, maybe; the rest was what it had*
  ("a Flask with its hand in everybody's pocket"). Not a Barrel. The Breach remains
  reserved.

## The Caul Hill fight (D+48), as staged

**Terrain:** room 1 (above). The door is in the north wall; the corridor outside runs
east–west. The lifter lies over the carrier and sledge by the east-wall boxes, its hump
toward the corner heap. The floor rope enters over the threshold; the ceiling rope
through the hole above the door and down the inside wall; three arm-ropes lie out across
the boxes and floor. Heaps: the south-east corner; by the far cabinet; and in the
corridor by the steel door. The counter is on Dee's shoulder (off Callie's hip).

**Positions:** Callie just inside the threshold facing the corridor, hook still on the
threshold, lee up into the corridor and leaned back up the wall over the hole — **a door
and a bit**, with her back to the room. Dee by the cabinets near the door (glass, open
jar, vent-jar bag on a cabinet top). Jab crouched and moving, frame on a cabinet top.
Toren between the lifter and the others. A lantern on the corridor floor lights Callie's
side; one hangs from the frame.

**Sequence (Dee's count; a three and ten ≈ one glass turn):**
1. On a ten: Toren cuts the **ceiling rope outside the hole**, then the **floor rope at
   the threshold** (heel drag, "like a sack of flour opened with a hook"). Dee and Jab in;
   Callie last; lee set. **Isolation.**
2. First three: the corridor stump and the ceiling stump push, heap against the lee's
   outside, and cannot cross. An arm-rope swings at Callie's back; **Toren's Guard strut
   takes it** ("a sack hitting a wall"). Two husks stand in the room. **Catch 1:** Jab's
   palm on the room-side floor stump — one push, a palm's worth, "and then nothing
   behind it". The stump does not grow.
3. Toren pins the cabinet husk on a strut; Jab kills the corner husk (hook of the
   fish-knife lit) and **catches the corner heap** at the next three — no husk stands.
   Principle stated: *it doesn't matter where; it's all out of the drum* → Dee: *pick the
   one.*
4. **The lifter adapts:** it pushes into the pinned husk to make it heavier; Jab catches
   at the husk's foot. Then it **throws** the second arm-rope at Jab; Toren holds **two
   struts** (husk and a roof over Jab and Dee). Then it pushes to mend the struck rope
   instead of the stump Jab is on (a wrong pick).
5. Toren cuts the near arm-rope; Jab catches at the stump; it stays cut.
6. **Catch 7 late:** the ten draws half the palm's worth back out of Jab and him "after
   it"; after that he calls *off* before Dee.
7. Callie **looks round once** (Rook's prediction); Jab says "Busy"; she turns back. A
   corridor husk is pressed against the outside of her lee; blood on her lip.
8. Jar 2 done; jar 3 opened ("last above the line"). The hump shrinks to knee height; the
   three stays open longer (five breaths) — pumping itself dry.
9. **The lifter drags itself along the floor stump toward the door** — toward the river
   on the other side of Callie. Toren drops the roof and sets it in front of the lifter,
   a pace from Callie's heels; it **leans** ("That's the cart"). His stone goes; he holds
   on his own.
10. "Jar": **the line.** Dee asks Jab "What do you feel?" ("It's missing… nearly dry…
    one more, maybe two"); reads 130, not red; "Say it." Toren's crossing; Dee's
    co-signature; **jar 4 opened under the line.** Callie: "I've got two turns. I've not
    got three."
11. The open stays open. Jab puts his palm **on the lifter itself** and takes the push
    and holds on through the open; Toren lets the husk go (it does not rise), lights a
    finger of the **point** off his own, and **puts it into the open vent** before the ten.
    It goes out "like a pinched wick" and falls apart into a knee-deep slope of loose
    grey. Jab comes off and jars his last palm's worth.

**Kills:** D+47 — the corner husk three times in room 1, one husk at the steel door, one
against Callie's Ward at the stair top. D+48 — the corner husk once (Jab), then the
lifter; every husk it had made came apart when it died. **No spawn reconstituted after
the kill.**

## Recovered boxes

- **Two live wheel boxes** in the lead carrier, lid in its groove, on the tailboard
  sledge, on the cart bed behind the home Flasks (loaded by tipping the cart).
  - Box 1: 110 at a hand, slide shut (room 1, D+47).
  - Box 2: 106 at a hand, slide shut (room 1, D+48).
- **Not taken:** the 140 box (slide rusted half-open). Other live boxes may lie in the
  unread part of room 1's heap and in rooms 2–11; nobody read further ("a quarter of an
  hour on her knees… she could not do it").
- The carrier's tongs are grey to the rings and were left in room 1 (not stated as
  brought out).

## Bodies at exit (D+49 morning)

- **Pell:** right ankle broken, splinted with the tailboard rail and canvas strips; the
  swelling pressed the strips and Dee loosened one; toes pink. Sits up on the cart. Cannot
  walk. Counted the sky for two days.
- **Rook:** the reopened back is **infected** ("the dog"): hot, red a hand's width round
  the stitches, shiny, not spreading since D+47 night. Fever highest D+47; cooler D+48;
  "holding" D+49. **Sowerby's fever powder: three papers used, one left.** Off. On D+48 he
  dragged two wrack poles onto the bridge on his knees ("That's not working. That's
  furniture"). Rides on the cart on his side.
- **Toren:** left forearm in its cuff, jarred twice in M4, **not used as a lever in M5**.
  Own reserve to his floor D+48; "a turn" D+49 morning, "two by tonight if you do
  nothing". Stone empty.
- **Callie:** **left ribs cracked — two, the same place** — falling at her floor onto her
  own still-fixed conduit (D+48); strapped D+49; no lifting heavier than a counter for a
  week; hook as a walking stick. Nosebleed D+48. Own reserve half D+49. Stone empty.
- **Jab:** **right palm: a coin-sized white, shiny, fixed patch on the heel** from the
  D+47 sink; red edge and a small blister by D+49; he can draw with the rest of the hand
  "round it". Arm warm to the elbow D+47, drawn. Palms warm after the D+48 solo draws,
  drawn cool by Dee D+49. Floor a finger; own about a hand. Did not go below his floor.
- **Dee:** **at her floor once** (D+48) after being the watch for every catch with nobody
  drawing her; **sick in the night** (vomiting, shaking — twenty turns in the room at
  110–130 undrawn); drawn by Jab. D+49: a finger over her floor; can watch, not draw
  "today". Says of it: "I'll not do it that way again." Rook: "Yes you will."
- **Duchess:** sound; eating; not taken across the river.

- **Cart** (whole, heading to the cut): three home Flasks; the carrier on the tailboard

## Knowledge boundaries at exit

- **Known and shown:** the lifter's draw/push rhythm; that it pushes into any hole,
  including a drawing hand; that stoppered jars are beyond it; that a lee in a door stops
  crawling ash but not rope, and a cut rope plus a lee in the cut isolates a room; that
  catching "on the push" drains it; that its husks were its hands; that when it died the
  ropes stopped; that the sky **lost its count** the same afternoon and falls now come
  at no three.
- **Not known:** whether the sky kept the lifter's time or the lifter kept the sky's
  ("I don't know which" — Jab; "I'd like to know which" — Pell); what the falls are or
  what is in the drum; whether there are others like it; whether the loose ash on the
  yard and in the wash has **stopped moving** (not checked on the page outside room 1 and
  the corridor — Movement Six can show the drifts lying still, or not); how many live
  boxes remain in rooms 1–11.
- **Withheld:** Dee's door count; Rook's third door; Katori's words; the disk (Toren's
  stone is not mentioned in M5; **the single disk clue is held for Movement Six**); the
  Makers; Cinder.
- **The Director's permission:** kept as recorded above. The party never passed room 1.
  The count she asked for is on the paper (Callie's and Pell's).

## Route-home decision

Pell's options from D+46 still stand (north round the drifts' head toward the hill; or
south through the marsh and down this bank — two days more either way, no bed for the
cart; home about **D+57 or later**). On D+49 Pell turns his own "Nothing takes a cart
across that" into **"A cart in pieces goes across in pieces"**: wheels rolled down and up
the gap, the axle carried by two, the bed by four or two and the horse on a rope, the
shafts one each, the carrier's board slid over the drift ("it's flour"), Toren's strut
steps on the loose banks, Pell on a canvas litter, Duchess led through. Callie prices it:
**an hour at ~80, all of them and the horse, about 10–12 of the lifter's**. Toren: **the
wash, in pieces, tomorrow at first light if the wind is down; not today** ("Nobody
carries a wheel through a drift at their floor"); if the wind is up, wait at the cut and
write the cost. Dee writes it. They move the whole cart a mile to the cut on D+49. Home
**D+55** if the crossing goes on D+50 and the road holds (five days from east of the gap).
**The crossing itself is not drafted.**

## Open clocks and exact exit pressure for Movement Six

- **Where and when:** the cut on the west side of the collapsed wash crown, D+49
  afternoon/evening; the crossing planned for first light D+50, wind permitting.
- **Machine:** the next cycle is about **D+54**; the party cannot be home before
  **D+55**. "Nothing's the fifty-fourth. The fifty-fourth was gone before we crossed the
  bridge." Sowerby can dose Senna off her blood "for a few cycles. Badly." The part is
  on the cart; the repair is not done.
- **Ash:** lifter's 20½ clean before the crossing (~10½ after), fence 13, dirty 4, home 24
  untouched. Nobody carries settled ash at exit. Toren must settle for his strut-steps.
- **Bodies:** Rook's infection with one fever paper left; Pell carried; Callie's cracked
  ribs; Jab's fixed palm mark and a blister; Dee a finger over her floor; Toren's own
  recovering, forearm for Sowerby.
- **The rip:** the sky over Caul Hill has **lost its count** and still lets go falls. Pell:
  the Director will want that "more than the boxes, I think. Or as much." Movement Six's
  Sag surge from the distant rip remains available and is not foreshadowed further.
- **Meridian (not visited):** reviews held; Wyck cleared D+56; the technician's hand
  known ~D+57; Tonk and Tilda as at D+41; Dessa's six blank lines.

## Deliberately absent or withheld

- **No new continuing named character.** "The lifter" is a creature term. The shed's
  label-writer is not named.
- **No home Flask spent. No infinite ash, no soul-theft, no time reversal, no universal
  healing, no second hidden power.** The lifter never reached a stoppered jar or a settled
  stone; Ward stopped matter, not radiation (the lee did nothing about the room's
  reading); Sustain drew active burden only — Jab's palm mark, Callie's ribs, Pell's
  ankle and Rook's infection all remain; Guard was paid by the breath each time.
- **No pre-death windfall:** the lifter's ash was lifted only after it died; the crossing
  under the line was paid from fence jar 4.
- **No romance. No death.** Rook and Pell live and stay injured.
- **The east road is still cut; the return, the machine repair, the accord, patient
  outcomes and the north-yard school are unresolved.**

## Length and viewpoint (facts for review, not scores)

- **Length:** 46,193 words (`wc -w`), inside the packet's approximate 42,000–48,000.
- **Viewpoint share, Movement Five:** Callie 36.9% (3 chapters, 17,057), Toren 35.4%
  (3, 16,358), Jab 27.7% (2, 12,778). **This does not finish "approximately even by
  words"**: Jab's two chapters needed to be about 2,500 words longer between them to
  reach a third. Recorded, not silently rebalanced; for the editorial pass.
- **Cumulative through Movement Five** (268,994 words): Callie 34.5%, Toren 33.4%, Jab
  32.0%.
- **Scene breaks:** 106 `---` markers across 8 chapters, about 436 words per section —
  short against the formula's ~950-word proxy, the same direction as Movements Two to
  Four.
- **Surface phrases watched:** "the way …" occurs about 139 times across the movement
  (including literal uses such as "the way home"), the same tic flagged and thinned in
  M4. "Nobody said anything" occurs 7 times. None of the M4 watched tics ("the woman
  with the good boots", "face did nothing at all, which was how", "tipped plate", "about
  four seconds", "grey-haired man") appears.
- **Formula comparison** (sentence distribution, Flesch, development-beat cadence,
  progression-vocabulary placement) was **not run**; it belongs to the editorial pass.
